The charge then tests resolve in motion: its truth is enacted by completion and its falsehood by stopping short.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Brow, Skin Mark, Beauty, and Facial Defect","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_07d7f4df7c48c4b77873","text":"A visible surface can display form, mark, polish, defect, decay, or ornament while concealing a different functional state.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Prop, Load, Strength, and Collapse","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0824dfade513f8dce110","text":"Bodies and implements remain serviceable through lower members, restraining fittings, handles, props, and load-bearing strength.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Office, Custom, and Civic Stewardship","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_082d3adc9b0cd668756e","text":"An appointed official maintains a settled community through recurring office, customary order, and active stewardship.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Fabrication and Rejection","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_086b07ff0cdd3a7e4865","text":"falsehood opposed to truth `ك ذ ب:B001/m01`, fabricated speech `خ ل ق:B007/m01`, rejection of error or counterfeit `ر د د:B003/m01`, discrediting a speaker `ك ذ ب:B002/m02`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Labor, Office, and Livelihood","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_08b45775b16b0720b7e3","text":"Manual labor and appointed office are two organized forms of service. Wage converts that service into livelihood, while the support sense of `ق و م` makes livelihood what keeps both body and social arrangement standing.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Familiar Presence Against Wild Absence","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_09cee8e68ac4f49bf040","text":"Human or tame presence occupies a place from which wildness, estrangement, or complete absence would otherwise withdraw.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Fabrication and Rejection","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0a20f24fc0edd6ed0005","text":"A false account is manufactured, exposed, and refused like a defective or counterfeit item.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Facing Side, Forepart, and Posteriority","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0a5e09341d1e18e77341","text":"The human-facing side fixes orientation from the participant's viewpoint, while the spear's forepart supplies a functional front. Lower, leeward, and posterior positions complete a directional frame that works across bodies, tools, wind, and sequences.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Penetrating and Defective Judgment","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0a9ac3d938733e3899c5","text":"right-reaching wisdom `ح ك م:B003/m01`, penetrating counsel `ب ع د:B008/m01`, weak opinion `ل ي س:B007/m01`, desirable goodness `ح س ن:B001/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Trained Work Animal and Lagging Failure","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0cc146ba6d207cb45ab5","text":"Training gives the animal a stable work disposition, strength carries that disposition into motion, and lag exposes reduced aptitude. Exhausted stopping is the terminal state when the work capacity can no longer be sustained.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Residence, City, and Civic Obedience","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0e0125147739df6cb967","text":"People remain in a settled place whose identity depends on residence, assembly, and obedience to authority.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Worship, Obedience, and Upright Disposition","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0e21e8cbfb3767a00ad6","text":"Inner disposition aligns with worship, belief, upright conduct, benefaction, and the possibility of religious return or rupture.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001449/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Uncut Continuity and Material Cessation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0ece64ceb5598cb8c371","text":"cutting or uninterrupted extension `م ن ن:B001/m01`, exhaustion that ends travel `م ن ن:B001/m02`, milk that does not persist `ك ذ ب:B006/m01`, cloth worn away through use `خ ل ق:B009/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Standing Support and Downward Return","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1010e669e8b56be09824","text":"Standing supplies the initial stable configuration, while support explains how that configuration persists. Return and downward direction then form a single reversal process in which what stood is placed back into a lower state.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Worship, Obedience, and Upright Disposition","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_13484ed087a85b41cf92","text":"Character supplies the inward formation, religion gives it an order of obedience, and worship directs it toward the divine. Straightness is the shared state in which inner disposition and enacted allegiance coincide.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Facing Side, Forepart, and Posteriority","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_14ca75beead794048841","text":"A body or implement is oriented by the side facing a person, its working forepart, its lower line, and what comes behind.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Combat and the Truth of a Charge","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_15218b2cf07b61dd8966","text":"Combatants resist one another with weapons, and a charge is judged by whether the attacker carries it through or falters.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Making and Activating","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_15783be74d5117539737","text":"Manufacture provides the object, application makes it operative, and rising to the affair supplies the active operator. Together they form a compact production-to-use sequence.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Celestial Station and Zenith","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_163846224cfe41b5cab9","text":"lunar station and empty sky-place `ب ل د:B004/m01`, moon as a named beautiful body `ح س ن:B004/m02`, sun standing at noon `ق و م:B017/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Faith, Good Work, and Beneficence","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1a9d11ffc3767b790587","text":"Inner disposition aligns with worship, belief, upright conduct, benefaction, and the possibility of religious return or rupture.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Kinship, Return, and Protective Honor","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1da5478acfa0b662c9d0","text":"95:4 `خلقنا`; Appearance and integrity therefore move in opposite directions.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Uncut Continuity and Material Cessation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_227a41ab43b4e44d3488","text":"Uncut extension names continuity positively through the absence of severance. Exhaustion, vanishing milk, and worn cloth provide three concrete ways in which motion, yield, and material presence reach an end.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Standing Support and Downward Return","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2394454023097c2fb36c","text":"An upright body or structure is sustained, returned from its established state, and directed into a lower position.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Faith, Good Work, and Beneficence","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_26267e2bde8dfc057718","text":"Faith supplies settled assent, work converts assent into action, and rectitude defines the action as sound. The same root that returns a thing to a prior state can therefore mark a departure from the adopted religious order.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Prop, Load, Strength, and Collapse","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_45493b1fb583f36d0da8","text":"The prop and standing support resist collapse, sustaining strength carries the imposed load, and load-bearing capacity describes the active bearer. Halt","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_798066c01288b80e0143","text":"Stature supplies the standing frame, strength lets it persist, and frailty reduces its capacity. Forbearance carries the same invariant into conduct: remaining under pressure without opening, breaking, or reacting.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Residence, City, and Civic Obedience","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_7cddec1d4297b1565746","text":"Residence converts land into an inhabited station, prolonged remaining stabilizes it, and civic obedience gives the settlement an ordered political form.
